## Stage P1.6: Recording and Parameter Provenance

Scope step 6. Parameter read, write, read-back and export, and full-run recording with provenance, before comparative runs rely on them (S11, S13, S16).

### TASK-159 — ArduPilot parameter service with read-back and metadata

- **Status:** DONE — Claude; baseline c1026fe; 2026-11-01 (registered 2026-10-28 from PROJECT_SCOPE.md, baseline 384e974). Goal: a cold-path `ParameterService` that downloads the complete PARAM_VALUE list, writes with PARAM_SET and classifies each write by read-back, plus parameter metadata generated offline from the pinned ArduPilot source. Failure criterion: a list declared complete with an index missing, a write reported `confirmed` that the autopilot does not hold at the exact float32, a write left unclassified, a reboot-required parameter not flagged, or a displayed value that does not parse back to the same float32. Plan, declared before the code: the service follows ArduPilot's handlers at 8ab6afde (`GCS_Param.cpp`); the list request is resent while nothing answers, missing indices are read by index after a quiet interval, a new param_count restarts a download and marks a completed list `stale`; a write sends PARAM_SET and then PARAM_REQUEST_READ by name and is decided by answers outside the list; the service refuses, without sending, what the list or the parameter type cannot carry; timeouts run on a caller-supplied clock, so a paused lockstep SITL (TASK-129) times nothing out; metadata comes from ArduPilot's own `param_parse.py`, keyed by commit, with upstream anomalies listed, not repaired; values are shown as the shortest decimal that parses back to the same float32. Touched Files additions, made before the code: `packages/autopilot/src/ground-station.ts`, `packages/autopilot/package.json`, `scripts/check-param-sitl.mjs` with its report `test-results/validation/param-sitl.json`, and the metadata row in `config/offline-inventory.json`, `docs/operations/offline-inventory.md` and `docs/ru/offline-inventory.md`. Two design faults found by the tests before any native run and fixed: a single differing answer refused a write, although answers to a write that a newer write of the same name replaced are still in flight (a differing value now refuses only when it is still the latest answer after a quiet retry interval, while the requested float32 confirms at once); and every list entry still in flight from a replaced stream restarted the download again (entries carrying the replaced count are now dropped). Implemented: `ParameterService` with download states `idle`, `downloading`, `complete` and `failed` and write states `requested`, `confirmed`, `refused` (by the service or the autopilot), `timeout` and `stale`; `formatParameterValue` and `parseParameterValue`; `GroundStation.paramSetInto` and `paramRequestReadIndexInto`; `scripts/generate-param-metadata.mjs`, which writes `data/ardupilot/param-metadata.json` (schema 1, 1,734,597 bytes, SHA-256 `1b0645bc…`) for 8ab6afde with 5,799 ArduCopter parameters and one anomaly (`TA_GSP_AIRBRAKE`, range low −1 above high −10, left out); and `parseParameterMetadata`. Specification: [SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md](SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md), section "ArduPilot parameter service (TASK-159)". Evidence (MacBook Air Apple M5, macOS 26.6, Node 24.22.1). Metadata: `node scripts/generate-param-metadata.mjs --source <launcher home>/sources/ardupilot --python <launcher home>/venv/bin/python` exit 0 (upstream output SHA-256 `260fb058…`, Python 3.9.6 with lxml 6.1.3); `--check` against the separate TASK-127 checkout gives `identical`, exit 0; a missing checkout exits 2 (`BLOCKED_EXTERNAL`); the source checkout stays clean. Unit: `npx --no-install vitest run tests/param-service.test.ts tests/integration/param-sitl.test.ts tests/parameter-readback.test.ts` — 39 passed, none skipped (the pymavlink 2.4.50 oracles included), with 1,000,000 sampled float32 bit patterns formatted and parsed back exactly. Native: `node scripts/check-param-sitl.mjs --home <TASK-152 launcher home>` exit 0, PASS, report `test-results/validation/param-sitl.json` (SHA-256 `2b71c7f0…`), stock ArduCopter V4.8.0-dev `8ab6afde` (binary `02066ec9…`, defaults `5e01345b…`) at 400 Hz JSON lockstep, the vehicle at rest. The full list of 1,410 parameters downloaded in 2.13 s of simulated time (68 ms of wall time), each index once, and the metadata covers all 1,410. Writes: `ATC_RAT_RLL_I` 0.00017 confirmed at float32 0.00016999999934341758 and shown as `0.00017`; `SERIAL1_PROTOCOL` 1 confirmed with reboot-required set; `BARO1_DEVID` refused by the autopilot, which kept 65540 (`AP_PARAM_FLAG_INTERNAL_USE_ONLY`); an unknown name and 1.5 for the INT8 `FRAME_CLASS` refused by the service without sending. A write of `PSC_D_POS_P` while the physics answers were held for 5.03 s of wall time stayed `requested` with simulated time unchanged and was confirmed after they resumed. `GRIP_ENABLE` 1 marked the completed list stale; the next download held 1,417 parameters with the gripper group, and every confirmed value read back in it. A third download that dropped the first arrival of every entry whose index ends in 37 lost 14 entries, sent 14 reads by index and completed equal to the second, except volatile parameters. Four more runs passed, and no ArduCopter process remained after any run. `tests/integration/param-sitl.test.ts`: the report judge rejects 22 kinds of false evidence, and a host without an installed firmware is `BLOCKED_EXTERNAL` without a report. `node scripts/check-offline.mjs`: PASS, after the metadata row became ready with its file hash and the three documentation links inside ArduPilot's parameter descriptions were listed. `tests/offline-inventory.test.ts` now expects four hashed files and the metadata row ready (added to Touched Files with that change). Full gates: `npm test` — 1233 passed, 1 skipped, 3 failed: the offline-inventory expectation above, fixed and passing alone (11 of 11); the known `drive-chain` golden-hash difference of this Mac; and `tests/contact-binding.test.ts`, which timed out at 5 s while another build ran and passes alone (10 of 10). `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm run check:docs` and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Title & Description:** (S11) Download the full parameter list with index and count tracking and retries. Write with PARAM_SET and confirm by read-back, distinguishing requested, refused, confirmed and timed-out values. Generate parameter metadata (units, ranges, groups, reboot-required) offline from the pinned ArduPilot source of each supported version. Show values at full precision, for example `0.00017`, never `0.00`.
- **Dependencies:** TASK-150, TASK-153.
- **Touched Files:** `packages/autopilot/src/param-service.ts`, `packages/autopilot/src/param-metadata.ts`, `scripts/generate-param-metadata.mjs`, `data/ardupilot/param-metadata.json`, `tests/param-service.test.ts`, `tests/integration/param-sitl.test.ts`, `packages/autopilot/src/ground-station.ts`, `packages/autopilot/package.json`, `scripts/check-param-sitl.mjs`, `test-results/validation/param-sitl.json`, `config/offline-inventory.json`, `docs/operations/offline-inventory.md`, `docs/ru/offline-inventory.md`, `tests/offline-inventory.test.ts`, `docs/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/ru/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`, `docs/ru/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`.
- **Interfaces / Contracts:** Parameter states `requested`, `refused`, `confirmed`, `timeout` and `stale`; float32 wire values stored exactly; metadata keyed by ArduPilot commit; the reboot-required flag taken from metadata.
- **DoD & Acceptance Criteria:** The full list downloads from native SITL. Every write's outcome is confirmed by read-back or classified. Reboot-required parameters are flagged. Value formatting round-trips the exact float32.

### TASK-160 — Parameters tab: editing, history and parameter sets

- **Status:** DONE — Claude; baseline 1d57e08; 2026-11-02 (registered 2026-10-28 from PROJECT_SCOPE.md, baseline 384e974). Goal: the Parameters tab reads the actual parameters of the Run tab's SITL and writes them before and during flight and while paused, with every write's state, the restart it needs, a change history kept with the experiment, and parameter sets that are saved, compared and sent back. Failure criterion: a restart-required change labelled applied, a write while paused that is retried, timed out or lost, a change missing from the history or lost on reload, or a set restore that writes read-only parameters. Design: the session controller loads a run's parameters through the TASK-159 `ParameterService`, which replaces its own list requests (gap recovery reads missing entries by index, and a failed recovery requests the list again within the start's 30 s deadline); the tab shares the Run tab's session through `SessionLink`; the browser fetches the parameter metadata as a separate asset; the project store gains the `parameter-set` and `parameter-log` record kinds, each depending only on an experiment; a SITL that MirageSITL did not start stays read-only, following the decision of 2026-11-01 that MirageSITL writes nothing to it. No change needs a disarm: ArduPilot 8ab6afde's PARAM_SET handler (`GCS_Param.cpp`) has no armed check, so the tab shows only the restart that the metadata declares. A timeout cannot be provoked from a real ArduPilot, which answers every listed parameter; the scripted controller test covers it. Found by this task's browser test and fixed in the TASK-159 service: ArduPilot answers reads by name from a queue, so after a paused SITL resumed with two writes of one parameter waiting, the answer to the earlier read arrived after the later write's confirmation and marked it stale although the autopilot held its value; a contradicted confirmed write is now read once more and is stale only when the latest answer still differs after `writeRetryS` without an answer, and a newer confirmed write of the same name replaces it at once. Touched Files beyond the registered list: `apps/web/src/params/metadata.ts`, `apps/web/src/params/log-writer.ts`, `apps/web/src/run/session-link.ts`, `apps/web/src/run/session-controller.ts`, `apps/web/src/run/RunPanel.tsx`, `apps/web/src/App.tsx`, `apps/web/src/shell/AppShell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/style.css`, `packages/project/src/project.schema.json`, `packages/project/src/store.ts`, `packages/autopilot/src/param-service.ts`, `tests/param-service.test.ts`, `tests/run-controller.test.ts` and both languages of `SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md` and `CLAUDE.md`: the tab needs the run's session and a place for its records, and the service fix needed its own tests. Evidence (MacBook Air Apple M5, macOS 26.6, Node 24.22.1, Playwright 1.56.1 Chromium): `tests/run-controller.test.ts` 103 passed, five new cases being the download and a write in flight confirmed, applied and logged with its old value, wall time and tick; a restart-required write pending a restart; refusals by the service and by the autopilot and a write nobody answers; a write while paused neither resent nor timed out, made stale by a newer one, which is confirmed after Resume; and the refusals before the list is loaded, while it is renumbered (then downloaded again), for a connection and after the run stops. `tests/param-service.test.ts` 24 passed, including the late queued answer. `tests/parameter-sets.test.ts` 5 passed. `tests/browser/parameters-tab.spec.ts` with the TASK-152 installation of ArduCopter 8ab6afde, through the application alone (1 passed, 1.3 min): a list of 1410 parameters; before flight `RTL_ALT_M` = 20 confirmed and applied, `GPS1_DELAY_MS` = 10 stored but not applied until a restart and listed as pending one, 10.5 refused before sending (an integer from -32768 to 32767), `BARO1_DEVID` = 1 refused by ArduPilot, which kept 65540 (read-only in the metadata); the set "Before flight" saved; in flight at 5 m, 25 confirmed; while paused, 30 stayed requested for 5 s, 35 made it stale, and 35 was confirmed after Resume; the set compared with the SITL showed `RTL_ALT_M` 20 against 35; sending the set wrote 1 value and skipped `STAT_DISTFLWN`, `STAT_FLTCNT`, `STAT_FLTTIME` and `STAT_RUNTIME` as read-only, and `RTL_ALT_M` was 20 again; `GPS1_DELAY_MS`'s old value 0 was sent back from the history and confirmed; after the run stopped, the stored `parameter-log` record held all 9 changes with the same outcomes as the tab, and after a page reload they were shown under Earlier runs. Full gates (same machine): lint of the changed files, typecheck and build 0, with the metadata a separate `param-metadata-*.json` asset of the build; `node scripts/check-offline.mjs --dist apps/web/dist` 0; `npm run check:docs` passes; `npm test` 1323 passed and 1 skipped, its failures `tests/drive-chain.test.ts`, which fails on main on this Mac too, and `tests/documentation-policy.test.ts` before the mirror hashes were refreshed, after which it passes (6 of 6); `npm run test:browser` 121 passed and 16 skipped (36.9 min) with one failure, `tests/browser/simulation.spec.ts` (the synthetic demo did not take off within its time budget at a load average of about 10), which passes 5 of 5 when run alone.
- **Title & Description:** (S11) Read the actual SITL parameters and edit them before and during flight, and on pause where supported. Show requested, refused and confirmed values and the disarm or restart each change needs. Record every change with its name, old and new value, time, outcome and experiment. Save and compare parameter sets and send earlier values back. Storage confirmation is never presented as applied behavior when a restart is needed.
- **Dependencies:** TASK-137, TASK-156, TASK-159.
- **Touched Files:** `apps/web/src/params/ParametersTab.tsx`, `apps/web/src/params/ParameterHistory.tsx`, `apps/web/src/params/parameter-sets.ts`, `apps/web/src/params/metadata.ts`, `apps/web/src/params/log-writer.ts`, `apps/web/src/run/session-link.ts`, `apps/web/src/run/session-controller.ts`, `apps/web/src/run/RunPanel.tsx`, `apps/web/src/App.tsx`, `apps/web/src/shell/AppShell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/style.css`, `packages/project/src/schema.ts`, `packages/project/src/project.schema.json`, `packages/project/src/store.ts`, `packages/autopilot/src/param-service.ts`, `tests/parameter-sets.test.ts`, `tests/param-service.test.ts`, `tests/run-controller.test.ts`, `tests/browser/parameters-tab.spec.ts`, `docs/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/ru/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`, `docs/ru/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/ru/CLAUDE.md`.
- **Interfaces / Contracts:** Parameter-change event: name, old value, new value, request time, outcome, simulated tick and experiment ID. A parameter set is a versioned record with its source.
- **DoD & Acceptance Criteria:** A browser test against SITL covers every state, including a write while paused or its documented refusal. Restart-required changes are never labeled applied. History persists with the experiment.

### TASK-161 — ArduPilot parameter file export

- **Status:** DONE — Claude; baseline f5d1e0d; 2026-11-03 (registered 2026-10-28 from PROJECT_SCOPE.md, baseline 384e974). Goal: at any stage, an ArduPilot parameter file for loading by hand, of all parameters, the changes against a baseline, or chosen parameters and groups, previewed with each value's source, with the aircraft, firmware and experiment context, and the simulator-only settings saved separately. Failure criterion: an exported file that ArduPilot does not load back to the same values, a pending write exported as a value, a selection that differs from its oracle, a file without its context, or anything written to a flight controller. Design: `formatParamFile` writes `NAME,VALUE` lines by name with the shortest decimal that reads back to each float32, after a `#` context header; values come from the running SITL's read-back (only what ArduPilot reported, so a pending write is named in the preview and never exported), an experiment record's parameters generated from its saved profile, or a TASK-160 parameter set; the baseline of a changes export is a stored parameter set, and a group is a name up to its first underscore; `ProjectStore.exportItems` writes the aircraft profile with its whole dependency closure as a project archive. Weather and location settings do not exist yet (TASK-148, TASK-167), so the simulator settings hold the aircraft geometry only. The rule for the native round trip was fixed before its first run: every exported value must read back as the same float32 except parameters ArduPilot's metadata marks read-only or volatile, which the autopilot sets itself. Evidence (MacBook Air Apple M5, macOS 26.6, Node 24.22.1, Playwright 1.56.1 Chromium): `tests/param-file.test.ts` 7 passed, with 200,000 sampled float32 bit patterns read back exactly and pymavlink 2.4.50's `MAVParmDict.load` reading 5,000 exported values bit for bit; `tests/integration/param-file-sitl.test.ts` 2 passed, its judge rejecting 10 kinds of false evidence. `node scripts/check-param-file-sitl.mjs --home .cache/task-152/home` exit 0 (`test-results/validation/param-file-sitl.json`): SITL A of ArduCopter 8ab6afde confirmed `ATC_RAT_RLL_I` 0.00017, `RTL_ALT_M` 20.25, `LAND_SPD_MS` 0.75 and the reboot-required `GPS1_DELAY_MS` 10 and exported its 1410 parameters (24,095 bytes); SITL B, started in an empty directory from `copter.parm` and the exported file, listed 1410 parameters, and all 1377 compared values read back as the same float32; of the 33 parameters the autopilot sets itself, only `STAT_BOOTCNT` differed. `tests/browser/parameter-export.spec.ts` against native ArduCopter through the application alone (1 passed, 48 s): with `LAND_SPD_MS` 0.75 pending while paused, the all-parameters file held 1410 lines with ArduPilot's confirmed value and the preview named the pending write; the header named the aircraft, the firmware commit and the experiment record; the chosen file (2 parameters and the group RTL) held 10 lines and the changes against the stored set exactly `GPS1_DELAY_MS` and `RTL_ALT_M`, both equal to their oracles; an experiment record exported its generated frame, output and PWM parameters at the values the SITL reported; and the simulator settings were a 6-record project archive of the project, the profile and its 4 component cards. Full gates (same machine): lint of the changed files, typecheck and build 0; `node scripts/check-offline.mjs --dist apps/web/dist` 0; `npm run check:docs` passes; `npm test` 1328 passed and 6 skipped, its failures `tests/drive-chain.test.ts`, which fails on main on this Mac too, the race of two `scripts/build-wasm.mjs` runs in `tests/accident-session.test.ts`, and two allocation tests (`tests/contact-allocation.test.ts`, `tests/links-ew-allocation.test.ts`) that ran 13 to 28 min while the Mac slept with its lid closed (power log: 01:06 to 01:34); run again alone, these three files pass 17 of 17; `npm run test:browser` 123 passed and 16 skipped (34.1 min), none failed.
- **Title & Description:** (S11) At any stage, export an ArduPilot-compatible parameter file for the user to load manually. It contains all available parameters, the changes relative to a selected baseline, or selected parameters and groups. Before export, preview the contents and each value's source: saved profile, experiment, or actual SITL read-back. Pending and unconfirmed requests are not exported as confirmed live values. The aircraft, firmware and experiment context accompanies the file. Simulator-only geometry and weather settings are saved separately. Nothing is written to a real controller.

### TASK-162 — Experiment recorder v2 with full-run streaming and per-signal rates

- **Status:** DONE — Claude; baseline c1026fe; 2026-11-01 (registered 2026-10-28 from PROJECT_SCOPE.md, baseline 384e974). Goal: recording format v2 with a signal catalog, per-signal effective rates, a producer fan-out in the physics Worker, a recorder Worker that streams checksummed chunks to IndexedDB without the 32 MiB cap of TASK-076, and records that reopen after a restart. Failure criterion: a sample count that does not match its effective rate, a duplicated sample, an effective rate above the source rate, omitted data that a reader can take for zero, a lost row without an explicit gap, an allocation or a wait on storage in the producer, a chunk whose corruption goes undetected, or a record that does not reopen after a restart. Plan and defaults, declared before the code (lead, open to change). Signals and native rates: truth (position, velocity, attitude, body rates, acceleration, specific force), per-motor speed, currents, power and command, battery, actuator outputs, pilot input and IMU observations all come from the physics tick at the physics rate (400, 800 or 1200 Hz; A-04); autopilot telemetry (ATTITUDE, LOCAL_POSITION_NED, GLOBAL_POSITION_INT, GPS_RAW_INT, EKF_STATUS_REPORT) arrives on Main at its stream rate and has no fixed source rate; events (parameter snapshot and changes, weather, fault, pause, accident, gap) are always recorded. Rates: one common rate (default 50 Hz, A-09) with per-group and per-signal overrides of 1 to 2000 Hz; the effective rate of a physics signal is min(requested, source), kept with an integer accumulator (keep a tick when the running sum of the requested rate reaches the source rate), so a run of N ticks keeps exactly floor(N·effective/source) samples, none twice; a request above the source rate, such as 2 kHz on a 1200 Hz source, is capped to the source rate and the plan marks it capped with both rates; a telemetry signal keeps a message when at least 1/requested seconds of simulated time passed since the last kept one. Format v2: a JSON header (schema version 2) with the signal table (ID, name, group, channel names and units, value type, source rate, requested and effective rate, capped) and the run's identity, then binary chunks with a fixed header (magic, version, record ID hash, chunk index, row count, first and last tick, payload bytes, CRC-32) and rows (signal ID, presence and gap flags, tick, simulated time, values); a row exists only for a kept sample, so an absent row is no sample, never a zero, and a row's presence mask marks channels the source did not produce; producer drops become gap rows with the exact count of lost rows. Transport: the producer in the physics Worker writes one fixed 512-byte row slot per kept group into its own SPSC SharedArrayBuffer (1024 slots) with atomic words and counts the rows it drops when the queue is full, never waiting; it is the second fan-out of the existing session record hook, so the flight queues keep one consumer. Storage: one persistent IndexedDB database `mirage-recordings` with a record catalog (status recording, closed or interrupted, chunk count, bytes, gaps) and chunks keyed by record and index; each chunk and the catalog entry are written in one transaction; a record left `recording` when the page restarts reopens as `interrupted` with every chunk written before. Touched Files additions before the code: `packages/aar/src/recorder-v2.worker.ts` and `packages/aar/src/recorder-v2-host.ts` (the recorder Worker and its Main-side host), `packages/aar/package.json` (exports, and `@mirage/mavlink` for telemetry decoding), `tests/support/recorder-v2-harness.ts` (browser harness; the planned `.html` page was not needed, because the harness runs on the cross-origin-isolated application page), and the reports `test-results/bench/aar-v2-400.json`, `-800.json` and `-1200.json`. Out of this task: the Logging tab and its size estimates (TASK-163), wiring the Run tab's controller and the parameter service to start a recording and send its events (TASK-163, TASK-165), and DataFlash capture (TASK-164). Implemented: the signal catalog of 30 signals with fixed IDs and `planRates`; format v2 (JSON header with the signal table, 40-byte row header, 48-byte chunk header with CRC-32, `decodeChunk` checking every row against the catalog); `RecordingProducerV2` with recording queue v2 (345,152 bytes, 2048 slots of 168 bytes, kept and absent counters per signal; the declared 1024 slots of 512 bytes became 2048 slots sized to the largest row, twice the rows in two thirds of the memory); `RecordingConsumerV2` with gap rows; `StreamWriter`, the persistent IndexedDB backend, a memory backend, `recoverInterrupted` and `openRecording`; the recorder Worker holding a Web Lock per record; `RecorderV2Host` with telemetry decoding, parameter rows and events; and the physics Worker's `recording-v2.arm` and `recording-v2.stop`. Specifications: [SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md](SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md), section "Recording format v2 (TASK-162)", and [SPECS_SHARED_MEMORY.md](SPECS_SHARED_MEMORY.md), section "Recording queue v2 (TASK-162)". One defect found by G1 and fixed before evidence: the producer's first version passed float values to small helper functions and computed a mask with `2 **`, and when V8 did not inline them it boxed the doubles (7 to 15 collections per million ticks; a probe with zero-valued state had hidden it); values are now written with DataView setters directly, the masks with integer shifts, and value and zero-fill loops are separate. Evidence (MacBook Air Apple M5, macOS 26.6, Node 24.22.1, Google Chrome 156). `npx --no-install vitest run tests/recorder-v2.test.ts`: 11 passed (14 s). The one-hour run at 400 Hz (1,440,000 ticks) checked every stored chunk's CRC and rows and found exactly floor(ticks · effective / source) rows per signal in strictly increasing ticks: `motors.speed` requested at 2 kHz, capped to 400 Hz, 1,440,000; `battery` at 7 Hz, 25,200; `truth.velocity` at 50 Hz, 180,000; `sensors.imu` at 100 Hz, 360,000; autopilot telemetry kept at 10 Hz, 36,000; a 1,410-parameter snapshot; and 60 pause events. Nothing was lost or dropped, and the record took 450.2 MB for the hour, within the A-08 budget of 2 GB. The A-09 default (every signal at 50 Hz, 8 motors, an IMU frame every tick) takes 234.9 MB per simulated hour. `node scripts/bench-aar.mjs 400 v2`, `800 v2` and `1200 v2` (reports `test-results/bench/aar-v2-400.json`, `-800.json` and `-1200.json`): PASS, with the producer at its worst case (every physics signal at the physics rate, 16 motors and an IMU frame every tick, 16 rows per tick, the queue drained in the same step) and every field fractional, 0 collections in all six windows, 0 bytes per step, 208,000,000 rows, none lost, the allocating control detected (35 collections), about 7.9 µs per tick for producer and drain together. The TASK-076 mode still passes at 400, 800 and 1200 Hz; its reports `test-results/bench/aar-400.json`, `-800.json` and `-1200.json` were regenerated because the bench script changed. `tests/browser/recorder-v2.spec.ts` (Google Chrome through `CHROMIUM_PATH`; Vite on port 5183 with a local, uncommitted copy of the Playwright configuration, because another checkout's Vite held 5173): the application's physics Worker ran the demo session with the producer armed; the closed record held exactly floor(n · 50 / 400) rows for every 50 Hz physics signal against n `truth.position` rows at 400 Hz in consecutive ticks; no motor, battery or actuator rows, but absent counts of n and floor(n · 50 / 400) (the demo has no electrical drive); the 2 kHz motor request capped to 400 Hz in the stored header; the parameter snapshot read back as float32; no missing or corrupt chunk. A second record that was left open when the browser closed reopened, after a relaunch on the same profile, as `interrupted` with at least two chunks and its rows from tick 1 without a gap. Physics Worker regressions in Chrome: `tests/browser/aar.spec.ts`, `runtime-v2.spec.ts`, `power-panel.spec.ts` and `simulation.spec.ts` pass. In a combined run under load, the runtime-v2 stale-epoch test and the simulation fixed-dt test each failed once on their input-freshness timing; both pass alone, and the fixed-dt test passed 5 of 5 times on this branch and 5 of 5 on the baseline. Full gates: `npm test` — 1218 passed, 1 skipped, 5 failed in two files: the known `drive-chain` golden-hash difference of this Mac, and four `tests/run-controller.test.ts` real-launcher tests whose hooks timed out at 10 s under load, which pass alone (89 of 89). `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm run check:docs` and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Title & Description:** (S13) Build a signal catalog with each source's native rate: truth, per-motor RPM, current and power, battery, sensor observations, actuator outputs, autopilot telemetry including the EKF estimate, and events. Recording uses a common rate with per-signal and per-group overrides. The effective rate is the lower of the requested and source rates, without duplicated samples, and 2 kHz is offered only for sources that produce it. Record the parameter snapshot and change events, and weather, fault, pause and accident events. Stream to local storage without the 32 MiB cap of TASK-076, with explicit gaps. Store channel, rate and availability metadata so that omitted data is never read as zero.
- **Dependencies:** TASK-076, TASK-136, TASK-153.
- **Touched Files:** `packages/aar/src/format-v2.ts`, `packages/aar/src/signal-catalog.ts`, `packages/aar/src/stream-storage.ts`, `packages/aar/src/recorder-v2.ts`, `packages/aar/src/recorder-v2.worker.ts`, `packages/aar/src/recorder-v2-host.ts`, `packages/aar/package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `apps/web/src/physics.worker.ts`, `tests/recorder-v2.test.ts`, `tests/browser/recorder-v2.spec.ts`, `tests/support/recorder-v2-harness.ts`, `scripts/bench-aar.mjs`, `test-results/bench/aar-v2-400.json`, `test-results/bench/aar-v2-800.json`, `test-results/bench/aar-v2-1200.json`, `test-results/bench/aar-400.json`, `test-results/bench/aar-800.json`, `test-results/bench/aar-1200.json`, `docs/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/ru/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/SPECS_SHARED_MEMORY.md`, `docs/ru/SPECS_SHARED_MEMORY.md`, `docs/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`, `docs/ru/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`.
- **Interfaces / Contracts:** Format v2 with a versioned header, signal table and chunk checksums. Fan-out happens at the producer boundary with one consumer per queue. Physics never waits on storage.
- **DoD & Acceptance Criteria:** A one-hour simulated run, accelerated in the test, records continuously within the storage budget. Sample counts prove the effective rates. A 2 kHz request on a 1200 Hz source is capped and the cap is shown. G1 holds at the producer. The record reopens after a restart.

### TASK-163 — Logging tab with size estimates and storage monitoring

- **Status:** DONE — Claude; baseline 0669655; 2026-11-02 (registered 2026-10-28 from PROJECT_SCOPE.md, baseline 384e974). Goal: the Logging tab of S13 and, as TASK-162 left to this task, the Run tab's runs recording with its settings. Failure criterion: an estimate outside its declared tolerance, a rate the recorder cannot keep that is saved, a low quota that is not warned of before it runs out, a gap that is not shown, or a run whose first ticks or events go unrecorded. Defaults (lead, open to change): a 60-minute run length and a 500 MB reserve; warn when the run length needs more than is free beyond the reserve, when the free storage is below the reserve, and while recording when the rest of the run at the measured growth needs more than is free beyond it; the settings are global, in the store's local settings; a connection records from its experiment record on. "Warning thresholds are recorded in the experiment" is experiment payload schema 3 with `recording` (schemas 1 and 2 read as schema 3 without one). The Touched Files list ends with the files added in implementation, from `apps/web/src/logging/logging-settings.ts` on. Evidence (MacBook Air Apple M5, macOS 26.6, Node 24.22.1, Playwright 1.56.1 Chromium): `tests/size-estimate.test.ts` 3 passed: through the producer, the consumer and the stream writer, 120 simulated seconds of the default plan store 7,772,160 bytes against an estimate of 7,772,112 (0.0006 %), and 30 of 800 Hz truth with motors requested at 2 kHz (capped at 800) 23,817,120 against 23,815,872 (0.005 %), within the 0.5 % declared, with exact physics row counts; `tests/logging-tab.test.ts` 5 passed (the A-09 defaults, refusals of 2500 Hz, 0.5 Hz, an event and an unknown name, the settings' round trip, warnings before a run, below the reserve and at the measured growth, and the growth figures); `tests/run-controller.test.ts` 94 passed, the two new ones checking prepare before the record, start after it and before the bridge, the telemetry, snapshot, accident and pause calls, the close, and a recording that cannot start failing as `recording` with its fix; `tests/experiment-record.test.ts` 12 passed (schema 3's `recording` and seven refusals, schema 2 and 1 records read as schema 3 without one). `tests/browser/logging-tab.spec.ts`: the tab alone shows the estimate the module computes, refuses 2500 Hz and 1.5 Hz with the reason and disables Save, caps 2 kHz motors at 800 Hz, keeps the settings after a reload, fits 390 px, passes axe, and warns of a 600 MB quota with 50 MB used before any run (1 passed); with the TASK-152 installation of ArduCopter 8ab6afde a Run-tab run of the quad X records while the tab shows its growth per simulated hour and per real second with no gap, and after Stop the record is closed with no lost row, every physics signal holds exactly floor(ticks·50/800) rows (2,157 over 34,514 ticks), the 21 parameters are its snapshot, the experiment record names the record and the default settings, and the 2,713,080 stored bytes are 2.95 % below the estimate for the recorded time plus the snapshot, within the 10 % declared, since ArduCopter sent its three requested streams below 10 Hz (347, 330 and 290 rows) and ATTITUDE and LOCAL_POSITION_NED not at all (1 passed, 39 s). Full gates (same machine): lint, typecheck and build 0; `npm run check:docs` passes; `npm test` 1310 passed and 1 skipped, its only failure `tests/drive-chain.test.ts`, which fails on main on this Mac too; `npm run test:browser` 116 passed and 16 skipped (17.5 min), and one failure that this task caused and then fixed: on a connection to a SITL the user started, MAVLink already flows when the recording is prepared, and the recorder forwarded those rows before its Worker had opened the record, which refused them, so the connection failed as `recording` before READY; the run recorder now holds rows until its recording starts, and `tests/browser/manual-connect.spec.ts` (3 passed) and `tests/browser/logging-tab.spec.ts` (2 passed) pass again.

### TASK-164 — ArduPilot DataFlash capture per experiment

- **Status:** DONE — Claude; baseline 5d8f5bb (TASK-159 on c1026fe); 2026-11-01 (registered 2026-10-28 from PROJECT_SCOPE.md, baseline 384e974). Goal: the launcher checks an experiment's `LOG_*` parameters against the generated metadata (TASK-159) and SITL's logging capability before it starts SITL, and at the end of every run attaches each DataFlash file the run wrote, with its size, SHA-256, the ArduPilot version read from the log and the logging parameters read back from the log's PARM records. Failure criterion: a run whose `.BIN` is not attached or not hashed, logging reported as configured without the log's own PARM records showing it, a missing, empty, truncated or damaged log reported as complete, or a log attributed to the wrong run. Plan and defaults, declared before the code (lead, open to change). Checks: every `LOG_*` name must exist in the metadata of the firmware's commit, a value of an enumerated parameter must be one of its values, a bitmask may set only declared bits, a declared range bounds the value, and `LOG_BACKEND_TYPE` must include the File backend (bit 0, the only one whose output the launcher can collect) and must not request the Block backend (bit 2), which SITL does not have; a refused start names every reason and creates nothing. Attribution: the launcher lists `logs/` before each run starts; files that are new or grew belong to that run. Classification from the reader of TASK-178: `complete` (ends at a record boundary with no damaged range), `truncated` (only the tail after the last whole record is cut off, as when SITL is stopped by a signal; the cut bytes are counted), `damaged` (a skipped or bad-format range inside), `empty`, and `missing` (the run wrote no file). The attachment is the experiment folder's `dataflash.json`, rewritten atomically after each run and returned by `GET /v1/experiments/{id}/logs`; the experiment record already names the launcher experiment whose folder holds these files, so the project schema gains no kind in this task, because a new record kind needs a validator registered in the application shell, which other work is changing now (`packages/project/src/schema.ts` is therefore not touched). Touched Files additions before the code: `packages/autopilot/src/dataflash-logging.ts` (the checks, the read-back from PARM and VER, the classification), `packages/autopilot/package.json` (export), `apps/launcher/src/server.ts` (the start check and the capture at the end of a run) and `apps/launcher/package.json` if a dependency is needed. Implemented: `checkLoggingParameters`, `assessDataflash`, `confirmLogging` and `firmwareMatches` in `packages/autopilot/src/dataflash-logging.ts` (reading DataFlash with the TASK-178 reader, so `@mirage/autopilot` now depends on `@mirage/dataflash`; `package-lock.json` gains that one line); `snapshotDataflash`, `captureDataflash` and `writeDataflashManifest` in `apps/launcher/src/logs.ts`; and in `apps/launcher/src/server.ts` the start check, the listing before each run and the attachment once per run when it ends, returned as `attachments` by the logs route. Attribution counts a changed modification time as well as a new or grown file, because a run can rewrite a file at the same size (the shared fake SITL does). `apps/launcher/package.json` needed no change: the launcher imports these modules by relative path, as it does the bridge. Specification: [SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md](SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md), section "DataFlash capture per experiment (TASK-164)". Evidence (MacBook Air Apple M5, macOS 26.6, Node 24.22.1; every launcher, bridge and relay on a port the system chose, so no other session's Vite on 5173 or launcher on 47800 was touched). `npx --no-install vitest run tests/dataflash-capture.test.ts`: 7 passed, none skipped. The checks named all seven reasons of a refused configuration, among them bit 14 of `LOG_BITMASK`, which 8ab6afde does not declare, and `LOG_MAX_FILES` 1 outside 2 to 500. The real log slice of TASK-178 classified as complete with 2,259 records, firmware `ArduCopter V4.8.0-dev (8ab6afde)` with git hash `8ab6afde`, and 1,410 parameters read back, among them `LOG_BITMASK` 180222; cut by 7 bytes it was truncated (2,258 records); with a damaged range inside it was damaged. Through the launcher with the fake SITL, a start with `LOG_BACKEND_TYPE` 4 and `LOG_DISARMED` 9 was refused with both reasons and no experiment folder, and two runs each attached their 6-byte log as truncated, unconfirmed (`LOG_DISARMED` not in the log), with its SHA-256, in the response and in `dataflash.json`. With the TASK-127 build of ArduCopter 8ab6afde (binary `d47c1f64…`) through the launcher's own bridge, 16,002 lockstep frames at 800 Hz with `LOG_BACKEND_TYPE` 1, `LOG_DISARMED` 1, `LOG_BITMASK` 176126 and `LOG_FILE_DSRMROT` 0: the run attached `00000001.BIN`, 798,720 bytes, SHA-256 `f65d8efe…` equal to the file's, 16,316 records, truncated by 52 bytes as SITL was stopped by a signal, no damage inside, the VER firmware matching the build, and all four `LOG_*` values read back from its PARM records (`confirmed`). The real test passed in three runs. Regressions: `tests/launcher.test.ts`, `tests/run-controller.test.ts`, `tests/experiment-record.test.ts` and `tests/param-service.test.ts` — 138 passed; `tests/documentation-policy.test.ts`, `tests/offline-inventory.test.ts` and `tests/dataflash.test.ts` — 43 passed; `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm run check:docs` and `git diff --check` pass. The full `npm test` was not run for this task, at the user's request not to load the Mac that other sessions share; the suites above cover every file the task changed.
- **Title & Description:** (S13, S14) Configure and verify SITL DataFlash logging: the `LOG_*` parameters are checked against metadata and capabilities, not assumed arbitrary. Collect each run's `.BIN` files from the launcher's working directory into the experiment with hashes, kept separate from the MirageSITL recorder.
- **Dependencies:** TASK-151, TASK-154, TASK-159.
- **Touched Files:** `apps/launcher/src/logs.ts`, `apps/launcher/src/server.ts`, `packages/autopilot/src/dataflash-logging.ts`, `packages/autopilot/package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `tests/dataflash-capture.test.ts`, `docs/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/ru/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`, `docs/ru/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`.
- **Interfaces / Contracts:** Experiment attachment: file name, size, SHA-256, ArduPilot version and logging parameters. Missing and partial logs are reported, never synthesized.
- **DoD & Acceptance Criteria:** Every run's `.BIN` is attached and hashed. The configured logging is confirmed from the log contents. A missing or truncated log is reported.

### TASK-165 — Experiment lifecycle: Repeat, Create variant, export and import

- **Status:** DONE — Claude; baseline 48b4579; 2026-11-03 (registered 2026-10-28 from PROJECT_SCOPE.md, baseline 384e974). Goal: Repeat and Create variant of a stored experiment as new experiments that name their parent, the parent unchanged, and export and import that report what an archive cannot hold. Failure criterion: a repeat whose inputs differ from its parent, a variant whose listed changes are not its real ones, a changed parent, or an export or import that hides a missing dependency. User decision of 2026-11-03: reproduction is never judged; MirageSITL shows how a repeat or a variant differs from its parent and the user decides, so the contract's stored reproducibility tolerance is replaced by no tolerance. Design: experiment payload schema 4 adds `lineage` (original, repeat of a parent record's SHA-256, or variant with the changed inputs in a fixed order), which `createExperimentRecord` checks against the parent's inputs with `experimentChanges`; schema 3 records read as schema 4 originals. The Experiments tab plans the Run tab's next run through `SessionLink.planRun`: a repeat locks the parent's profile version, ArduPilot build, speed, physics rate, home and recording setup, a variant lets the profile version, the build and the speed change, and a variant that changed nothing is recorded as a repeat. Weather, location, the flight task and the fault schedule join the inputs when their tasks land. The registered `packages/project/src/experiment.ts` and `tests/experiment-lifecycle.test.ts` were not needed: the payload lives in `packages/aircraft/src/experiment.ts`, the lifecycle helpers in `apps/web/src/experiments/lifecycle.ts`, and the tests reuse the record fixtures of `tests/experiment-record.test.ts`. Evidence (MacBook Air Apple M5, macOS 26.6, Node 24.22.1, Playwright 1.56.1 Chromium): `tests/experiment-record.test.ts` 17 passed, the new cases being the schema 3 migration, the input changes and what is not an input, the refused false repeat and variant and a parent from elsewhere, the unchanged parent, an exported experiment imported into an empty store with its external dependencies, and the trajectory difference against a hand-computed oracle; `tests/run-controller.test.ts` 108 passed. `tests/browser/experiments-tab.spec.ts` with the TASK-152 installation of ArduCopter 8ab6afde through the application alone (1 passed, 1.7 min): an original run at 4x, its repeat loaded in the Run tab with the profile, build and speed locked, recorded the same `truth.position` at all 2079 common recorded ticks (no command reached the autopilot in either run), shown as identical and not judged; a variant at 2x was recorded as "Variant of ... : speed changed" and the original stayed an original; the repeat's archive, imported into another browser profile (7 records), listed its recording as not in that browser and its ArduPilot build as needed. Full gates (same machine): lint of the changed files, typecheck and build 0; `npm run check:docs` passes; `npm test` 1349 passed and 1 skipped, its failures `tests/drive-chain.test.ts`, which fails on main on this Mac too, the launcher-based cases of `tests/run-controller.test.ts` whose 10 s setup hook timed out under the full suite's load, `tests/external-sitl-flight.test.ts` and `tests/model-validation.test.ts`; run alone, those three files pass 113 of 113; `npm run test:browser` 126 passed and 16 skipped (36.0 min), none failed.
- **Title & Description:** (S16) An experiment fixes the aircraft version, the ArduPilot version and initial parameters, weather and location, the task, the fault schedule and the recording setup. It retains results and in-flight parameter, environment and fault events. **Repeat** reuses the same inputs; **Create variant** preserves the original while allowing changes. Reproducibility means preserved inputs and declared tolerances, not bit-identical outputs on every machine. Export and import report missing external dependencies.
- **Dependencies:** TASK-136, TASK-154, TASK-160, TASK-162.
- **Touched Files:** `packages/aircraft/src/experiment.ts`, `apps/web/src/experiments/lifecycle.ts`, `apps/web/src/experiments/ExperimentsTab.tsx`, `apps/web/src/run/RunPanel.tsx`, `apps/web/src/run/session-link.ts`, `apps/web/src/run/session-controller.ts`, `apps/web/src/App.tsx`, `apps/web/src/shell/AppShell.tsx`, `tests/experiment-record.test.ts`, `tests/browser/experiments-tab.spec.ts`, `docs/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/ru/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`, `docs/ru/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/ru/CLAUDE.md`.
- **Interfaces / Contracts:** Experiment record v1 references hashed inputs. A variant keeps a link to its parent. The reproducibility tolerance is stored with the experiment. The record keeps the TASK-143 sensor snapshot of its aircraft-profile version, and a variant takes the snapshot of its own profile version.
- **DoD & Acceptance Criteria:** Repeating a seeded run on the same machine matches within the declared tolerance. A variant leaves the original unchanged. An import into another store lists the missing dependencies.

## Stage P1.7: Flight Tasks, Weather, Faults and Batches

Scope step 7. Reproducible tasks, manual and scheduled effects, EKF-drift observations, run speed and user-defined batches (S07, S09, S10, S12).

### TASK-166 — Effect control contract for weather, sensor errors and faults

- **Status:** DONE — Claude; baseline feb91c9; 2026-11-05. Control Protocol v3 (`effect.set`, SPECS_SHARED_MEMORY section 6) carries a versioned record for 15 effects, read from one catalog whose ranges `tests/control-v3.test.ts` compares with the JSON Schema and, at each limit, with the owning model's own refusal. Off and zero intensity are bitwise identical to absent for all 8 effects a session can apply, over a seeded run through the real session on the BET quad with the production fault port, alone and beside effects that are on; the same control on changes the run, so the comparison is not vacuous. A `weather.set` flies bit for bit as its three migrated records on one tick, in any order, and a `fault.set` as its one. Mean wind, gust and air now switch separately: wind alone leaves the configured air in place. Scaling oracles: gust values exactly proportional on every tick, the stationary RMS within 3 % and the lag-one correlation within 0.005 of its formula, the ICAO troposphere for the air, the fix moved by the scaled offset through `GpsOffsetApplier`, and remaining thrust 1 − i·(1 − r). Scenario schema 2 schedules controls with an onset, a duration and a recovery; through `ScenarioBinder` every event is acknowledged applied on its own tick, and the effects change on those exact ticks. The instructor console has one form per effect from the catalog, in place of the presets. Evidence: `tests/control-v3.test.ts`, 56 passed; `tests/effect-controls.test.ts`, 36 passed; `tests/browser/instructor.spec.ts`, 3 passed in Chrome; `node scripts/check-bet-runtime.mjs --allocation`, exit 0, with `effect.set` among the commands of its session graph: −448 bytes over 1,000,000 steps, no collections, 750 commands applied. `npm test` on this tree: 1790 passed, with the known `tests/drive-chain.test.ts` hash difference on this Mac, and `tests/airframe-drag-sitl.test.ts` and `tests/effect-controls.test.ts` failing only while other files ran beside them; each passes alone. The second showed a race in `scripts/build-wasm.mjs` on a fresh checkout, now fixed. Limits of this task: a running session rejects the seven `sensor.*` controls visibly, since no run path can change a sensor error while it runs (the app's run path refuses MirageSITL-owned errors at start, and TASK-147 applies them only in the native driver), so their bitwise and scaling evidence is TASK-147's for the same model. The console stays on the demo page, where environment effects and `motor.loss` are rejected; TASK-167 and TASK-169 bring the controls to a real ArduPilot run, and TASK-168 delivers EW effects to it. TASK-106, TASK-107, TASK-121 and TASK-122 add their records when their models exist. The density limit an `atmosphere` control is refused at repeats three constants of the atmosphere model in the protocol package, which cannot import it; a grid test holds them equal.
- **Title & Description:** (S07, S08, S09) Give every environment, sensor-error, EW and fault effect a user-facing control: an on/off switch and an intensity setting, carried by a versioned control record with an identifier, an enabled flag, a dimensionless intensity, and physical parameters with units. Controls are set through the control protocol and the instructor console, apply at tick boundaries with acknowledgements, and can be scheduled with onset, duration and recovery. Every injected effect is off by default, and an effect that is off or at zero intensity is bitwise identical to an absent one. ArduPilot-side SITL errors keep the TASK-089 ownership setting, whose default is ArduPilot's own behavior. This task carries the user's request of 2026-10-27, first registered on the physics-review branch as an effect-control task numbered TASK-124; TASK-135 retired that number because main already uses it.
- **Dependencies:** TASK-010, TASK-075, TASK-079, TASK-089, TASK-093, TASK-156.
- **Touched Files:** `packages/protocol/src/control-v3.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/control-v3.schema.json`, `packages/protocol/src/control-v2.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/control-v2.schema.json`, `packages/protocol/src/index.ts`, `packages/physics/src/session-v2.ts`, `packages/physics/src/atmosphere.ts`, `packages/physics/src/wind.ts`, `packages/physics/src/bet-dynamics.ts`, `packages/scenarios/src/schema.ts`, `packages/scenarios/src/bind.ts`, `apps/web/src/instructor/Console.tsx`, `apps/web/src/instructor/commands.ts`, `apps/web/src/runtime.ts`, `apps/web/src/physics.worker.ts`, `apps/web/src/style.css`, `scripts/check-bet-runtime.mjs`, `scripts/build-wasm.mjs`, `tests/control-v3.test.ts`, `tests/effect-controls.test.ts`, `tests/control-v2.test.ts`, `tests/weather-plumbing.test.ts`, `tests/fixtures/protocol/control-v3.json`, `tests/fixtures/registry.json`, `tests/browser/instructor.spec.ts`, `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/ru/CLAUDE.md`, `docs/SPECS_SHARED_MEMORY.md`, `docs/ru/SPECS_SHARED_MEMORY.md`, `docs/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/ru/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/architecture.md`, `docs/ru/architecture.md`, `docs/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`, `docs/ru/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`.
- **Interfaces / Contracts:** A Control Protocol version bump with migration of v2 `weather.set` and `fault.set`, and rejection fixtures. `intensity` ≥ 0, where 1 is the configured nominal magnitude. Queued, applied and rejected acknowledgements. Every range equals the owning model's validity envelope, which resolves today's mismatches: turbulence RMS is 0–30 m/s in the protocol but 0–50 m/s in the gust model; temperature is 200–340 K against 180–330 K; wind is ±100 m/s against ±200 m/s per axis; and the protocol has no gust-bandwidth field. Covered effects: mean wind, gusts and turbulence (TASK-032, TASK-106); shear and terrain flow (TASK-107); thermals and hazard flows (TASK-121, TASK-122); atmosphere offsets (TASK-093); MirageSITL sensor errors (TASK-115–TASK-117, TASK-147); EW effects (TASK-053–TASK-056, composed by TASK-058); and motor faults (TASK-057). ArduPilot SITL errors are covered through the TASK-089 ownership setting. Effect tasks registered later declare their control under this contract, and a backend that cannot apply an effect keeps rejecting its control visibly.
- **DoD & Acceptance Criteria:** Every covered effect has a control record whose ranges equal its model envelope, and the existing `weather.set` and `fault.set` fields map onto these records. Migration and rejection tests and JSON fixtures pass. For every effect, disabled and zero intensity are bitwise identical to absent over a seeded run. Intensity scaling matches each model's definition against an independent oracle; for example, gust RMS scales linearly. The instructor console exposes every control with pending, applied and rejected status from the TASK-075 ledger, replacing today's hard-coded presets (north wind only, fixed EW magnitudes). Scenario files schedule controls with tick-bound acknowledgements (TASK-079), and scheduled effects apply on their exact ticks.

### TASK-167 — Weather tab with scheduled changes and repeatable gusts

- **Status:** IN_PROGRESS — Claude; baseline 340f8c3; 2026-11-05 (registered 2026-10-28 from PROJECT_SCOPE.md, baseline 384e974). Implementation (2026-11-05): `DiscreteGust` (`packages/physics/src/discrete-gust.ts`) adds its pulse to the wind of every tick after the turbulence; the catalog's `wind.discrete-gust` reduces to its peak NED velocity and duration, which the session writes into the WEATHER_DISCRETE_* slots, starting a new pulse on its own control and keeping a running one through any other weather change; the gust's per-axis streams (`GUST_STREAMS`) and Dryden's are stated once in `EFFECT_RANDOM_STREAMS` and recorded by `WeatherSchedule` (`packages/scenarios/src/weather-schedule.ts`, sharing `scheduledEntries` with the fault schedule); the run controller runs the weather schedule on the fault schedule's timeline from the same first arming; experiment payload schema 8 adds `weather` (an input after `conditions`, seeds included), and effect log schema 2 the parameters of each control; the Weather and location tab's weather section (`WeatherPanel.tsx`, `weather-form.ts`, `weather-store.ts`) applies each weather by hand or schedules it, and the Experiments tab names a record's weather. Evidence: `tests/discrete-gust.test.ts` 12 passed (every tick within 10⁻¹² m/s of the definition at 400, 800 and 1200 Hz, rate independence at common times, restart, keep, off and envelope, direction and intensity, and the wind the rotors see in a real session from the tick it takes effect, off and zero bitwise absent); `tests/weather-schedule.test.ts` 3 passed (the models' streams equal the recorded ones; a schedule of mean wind, gust, turbulence, discrete gust and air flown twice is bit for bit the same over 2400 ticks, and another gust or turbulence seed changes it); `tests/weather-form.test.ts` 5, `tests/effect-log.test.ts` 2, `tests/experiment-record.test.ts` 30 (schema 8, the schema 7 migration, the weather's refusals and lineage), `tests/run-controller.test.ts` 127 (a weather schedule's changes queued on arming + onset and logged with their values), `tests/control-v3.test.ts` 58 (the catalog against the JSON Schema and the gust's envelope) and `tests/effect-controls.test.ts` 40 passed; with `tests/fault-schedule.test.ts`, `tests/weather-plumbing.test.ts`, `tests/geographic-session.test.ts` and `tests/app-shell.test.ts`, 12 files and 318 tests passed. `tests/browser/weather-tab.spec.ts` 1 passed in Chromium (the section through the UI alone: a named refusal, an overlap refused, three entries with a new seed, axe without violations, the stored schedule with this build's streams, a reload and a removal). ### TASK-168 — Sensor dropout, frozen readings and link-loss effects

- **Status:** DONE — Claude; baseline feb91c9 with TASK-166 and TASK-106; 2026-11-05. Ten faults as TASK-166 records, each on the path that owns the reading (SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES, "Faults on the path to ArduPilot"): frozen IMU, GNSS and magnetometer on the state line, missing IMU, GNSS, barometer and magnetometer and frozen barometer through stock ArduPilot's fault parameters, pilot and ground-station link loss at the bridge. No firmware patch (D-10), since SITL makes its GNSS and magnetometer from the position and attitude MirageSITL sends. `tests/delivery-effects.test.ts`, 12 passed: truth is bitwise identical over the same servo frames with each sensor and link effect on, each freezes exactly its keys on exact ticks, and off is bitwise absent; `motor.loss` on the profile dynamics, in both modes, turns the body the way r × F says. `tests/run-controller.test.ts`: the parameter plan, its read-back status and its restore. Native stock ArduCopter, one flight per fault (`MIRAGE_SITL_FAULTS=1 npx vitest run tests/sitl-fault-effects.test.ts`, about 4 minutes, passed): every fault arrived, and `test-results/validation/task-168-faults.json` records ArduPilot's response, which no test predicts: a frozen IMU brought an EKF failsafe and a ground strike within 8 s; a missing GNSS, an EKF failsafe and LAND; a frozen GNSS went unnoticed while the aircraft moved 1.4 m; a missing IMU stops ArduPilot's main loop, and it does not come back. G1: `node scripts/check-bet-runtime.mjs --allocation`, exit 0, with a delivery effect among its session graph's commands: −432 bytes over 1,000,000 steps, no collections. Not done: `SessionController.setEffect` has no tab until TASK-169, and EW effects still stop at `EwEngine` on this path. The frozen GNSS holds north and east only, because height also feeds SITL's barometer.
- **Title & Description:** (S09) Add missing and frozen IMU, GNSS, barometer and magnetometer readings, delivered on each sensor's ownership path; for ArduPilot-owned sensors this uses catalog-verified fault parameters. Add loss of pilot control and loss of the ground-station link at the bridge. Wire reduced motor power and motor failure (TASK-057) into the profile dynamics. Observations and links change; truth does not.
- **Dependencies:** TASK-057, TASK-147, TASK-153, TASK-166.
- **Touched Files:** `packages/protocol/src/control-v3.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/control-v3.schema.json`, `packages/physics/src/session-v2.ts`, `packages/autopilot/src/fault-effects.ts`, `packages/autopilot/src/sitl-link.ts`, `packages/autopilot/package.json`, `apps/web/src/sitl/bridge-client.ts`, `apps/web/src/sitl/sitl-run.ts`, `apps/web/src/physics.worker.ts`, `apps/web/src/run/session-controller.ts`, `apps/web/src/instructor/Console.tsx`, `scripts/check-bet-runtime.mjs`, `tests/delivery-effects.test.ts`, `tests/sitl-fault-effects.test.ts`, `tests/run-controller.test.ts`, `tests/effect-controls.test.ts`, `tests/control-v3.test.ts`, `tests/browser/instructor.spec.ts`, `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/ru/CLAUDE.md`, `docs/SPECS_SHARED_MEMORY.md`, `docs/ru/SPECS_SHARED_MEMORY.md`, `docs/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/ru/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`, `docs/ru/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`.

### TASK-169 — Faults and interference tab with schedules and outcomes

- **Status:** DONE — Claude; baseline feb91c9 with TASK-182; 2026-11-05 (registered 2026-10-28 from PROJECT_SCOPE.md, baseline 384e974). Goal: a separate tab applies every S09 effect by hand or on a schedule and records what became of it, and the interference effects reach ArduPilot. Failure criterion: an effect reported applied that never reached ArduPilot, an effect that changes the truth, a schedule that drifts from the tick it names, or a refusal with no reason. The tab offers the `fault`, `delivery` and `ardupilot` families. A schedule (onset, duration, recovery) counts simulated seconds from the first armed report, and a run keeps it in its experiment record (payload schema 6, with a new comparison input); a repeat flies its parent's. Session effects are queued on their exact tick up to 5 s ahead, and parameter writes go when due. `effect-log` keeps each control's origin, the moment it was meant for, the moment it took effect, its outcome and what it wrote. GNSS step and drift reach ArduPilot on the state line, north and east, and a vertical component is refused because that height also feeds its barometer. Magnetometer rotation turns the attitude on the state line, and its bias is written to `SIM_MAGn_OFS`, added to the Sensors tab's value. Jamming writes `SIM_GPS1_NUMSATS`, `SIM_GPS1_ACC` and `SIM_GPS1_LCKTIME` from the same arithmetic as the kernel at its steady state, without the half-second low-pass. The real flights found three faults, all fixed: controls aimed at the next tick were refused as past at 4× speed, so a control is now aimed 0.25 s ahead of the tick read now and further ahead if refused; the schedule's settings key was too long to save; and TASK-168's `SIM_GPS1_ENABLE` hid its parameter group, which renumbered ArduPilot's list and refused the write that ended the effect, so a lost fix now uses the lock delay `SIM_GPS1_LCKTIME`. Evidence (MacBook Air Apple M5, macOS 27.2, Node 24.22.1, Google Chrome 156, native ArduCopter `8ab6afde`, the quad X at 4×): `tests/browser/faults-tab.spec.ts` 1 passed: nine scheduled classes, on and off, all applied, the session effects with the applied time equal to the time meant; one effect by hand on and off; a refused vertical step and an overlap with their reasons; a schedule that survives a reload; 21 entries in the stored experiment. `MIRAGE_SITL_FAULTS=interference npx vitest run tests/sitl-fault-effects.test.ts` 1 passed, its answers in `test-results/validation/task-169-interference.json` and in the table of SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES; the parameter list kept its length of 1410 through every flight. Unit: `tests/fault-schedule.test.ts` (the oracles are the jamming kernel at its steady state and SIM_Compass.cpp's arithmetic), `tests/delivery-effects.test.ts` (a step, a drift by its form and a rotation by independent matrices, with truth bit for bit), `tests/run-controller.test.ts`, `tests/experiment-record.test.ts`. G1: `node scripts/check-bet-runtime.mjs --allocation` 0 GC events in every graph, the session graph with these commands 864 bytes over 1,000,000 steps and 750 commands; `node scripts/check-sitl-link-allocation.mjs` 0.0031 B/step and 0 GC events over 1,000,000 steps with interference on the line. Remaining: the instructor console stays on the demo page; `sensor.*` errors are set on the Sensors tab and refused in a run; a run of more than about 9 h of simulated time would regain the fix that `sensor.gnss.missing` took away, because the lock delay holds no more.
- **Title & Description:** (S09) Add a separate tab that configures motor failure and reduced power, GNSS loss or degradation, GNSS position steps and gradual drift, magnetic interference, missing or frozen readings, and loss of pilot control or the ground-station link. Effects apply manually or on a schedule with onset, duration and recovery. Events and actual outcomes (pending, applied, rejected) are recorded.
- **Dependencies:** TASK-137, TASK-166, TASK-168.

### TASK-170 — EKF drift scenario with truth-versus-estimate charts

- **Status:** DONE — Claude; baseline feb91c9 with TASK-171; 2026-11-05 (registered 2026-10-28 from PROJECT_SCOPE.md, baseline 384e974). Goal: an EKF drift set up through the UI, flown as an input error, and its experiment showing the truth against ArduPilot's estimate. Failure criterion: a profile that departs from its form, a chart number that departs from the recording, a written estimate, or a promised response. `packages/scenarios/src/ekf-drift.ts` gives direction, growth rate, top speed, onset and duration, with gradual or abrupt removal, as stretches of the TASK-055 coherent drift on the TASK-169 schedule; a gradual removal brakes and runs back along the shortest path at the same rate, and ends at rest on the truth. `packages/analysis/src/truth-vs-estimate.ts` reads GLOBAL_POSITION_INT from the recording against the truth at the same simulated moment, and the Experiments tab charts position, velocity and their errors beside what was added to the input. Evidence (MacBook Air Apple M5, macOS 27.2, Node 24.22.1, Google Chrome 156, native ArduCopter `8ab6afde`, the quad X at 4×): `tests/ekf-drift.test.ts` 11 passed, declared tolerances first: position within two ticks at top speed and velocity within two ticks of acceleration, the closed form against the TASK-055 kernel stepped at 800 Hz through the schedule's own transitions, with nothing left at the end; `tests/browser/ekf-drift.spec.ts` 1 passed: a refused growth rate with its reason, all eight changes of its four stretches applied on their ticks, and the table held to arithmetic written out in the test from the recording's rows. In that flight ArduPilot followed its input: the truth's north velocity reached about -1.3 m/s against an added +1 m/s, and it stayed in GUIDED. That is what happened, not a promise. Remaining: a vertical drift is refused as in TASK-169; the estimate is reported about 8 times a second, so a chart does not resolve faster changes.
- **Title & Description:** (S09) Build the **EKF drift** input-error scenario on the TASK-055 coherent drift, delivered on the GNSS path. The UI configures direction, growth rate, duration, and gradual or abrupt removal. The scenario never overwrites ArduPilot's EKF or forces a trajectory. Charts compare the true trajectory and velocity with the EKF estimate from autopilot telemetry and show their errors. ArduPilot may drift, reject measurements or enter failsafe; its actual reaction is recorded.
- **Dependencies:** TASK-055, TASK-150, TASK-162, TASK-169.
- **Touched Files:** `packages/scenarios/src/ekf-drift.ts`, `packages/scenarios/src/fault-schedule.ts`, `packages/scenarios/package.json`, `packages/analysis/src/truth-vs-estimate.ts`, `packages/analysis/package.json`, `apps/web/src/analysis/TruthVsEstimate.tsx`, `apps/web/src/experiments/ExperimentsTab.tsx`, `apps/web/src/faults/FaultsTab.tsx`, `tests/ekf-drift.test.ts`, `tests/fault-schedule.test.ts`, `tests/browser/ekf-drift.spec.ts`, `tests/browser/faults-tab.spec.ts`, `docs/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/ru/SPECS_SIMULATION_ENGINES.md`, `docs/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`, `docs/ru/PROJECT_ROADMAP.md`.
- **Interfaces / Contracts:** Drift-profile parameters with units; estimate and truth aligned on simulated time; error series computed from the recording.
- **DoD & Acceptance Criteria:** The drift profile matches its analytic form. Chart errors match an independent computation from the recorded truth and estimate. No response is promised or hard-coded.

### TASK-171 — Refresh EW and fault regression evidence on macOS native SITL

- **Status:** DONE — Claude; baseline feb91c9 with TASK-169; 2026-11-05 (registered 2026-10-28 from PROJECT_SCOPE.md, baseline 384e974). Goal: the TASK-059 matrix on native ArduCopter on macOS, with the current EW sources and driver. Failure criterion: a validation capture outside the frozen limits, a calibration capture used to validate itself, or an earlier report changed. Six separate validation flights: `node scripts/report-ekf.mjs` PASS, `docs/validation/ew-macos-report.json`: control 0.09 m and innovation ratio 0.01, unhealthy rows 0; jamming 2.50 m, modes GUIDED and LAND; position step 30.00 m, ratio 8.26, the truth moved 0.12 m, and it stayed in GUIDED; velocity drift 70.13 m, ratio 10.10, the truth moved 74.68 m, modes GUIDED and LAND; magnetic 0.09 m, ratio 1.98; motor loss 2.10 m, ratio 2.52, the truth moved 13.54 m. These limits are regression limits of a synthetic quad, not measured fidelity or a promised response. `test-results/validation/ekf-report.json` and the archived Linux and WSL captures are unchanged, and the report goes beside them with `--output`. Remaining: none in Phase 1 for this matrix; a change to its 32 source files, its firmware or its parameters fails it closed and calls for a new freeze.
